           │ > You may recall the puzzle of a ferryman, goat, cabbage,    │
           │ and wolf all on one side of a river. The ferryman can cross  │
           │ the river with at most one passenger in his boat. There is a │
           │ behavioural conflict between: 1. the goat and the cabbage;   │
           │ and 2. the goat and the wolf; if they are on the same river  │
           │ bank but the ferryman crosses the river or stays on the      │
           │ other bank. We wish to know how the ferryman can transport   │
           │ all goods to the other side, without any conflicts           │
           │ occurring. Build a model in which the states represent which │
           │ goods are at which side of the river. Your model will need   │
           │ to have variables representing the location of the ferryman, │
           │ the goat, the cabbage and the wolf and a variable that       │
           │ indicates what is being carried in the ferry. Define a goal  │
           │ state in which all goods are on the other side of the river. │
           │ Write a property which checks whether this goal state is     │
           │ reachable without any conflicts occurring. Use the variable  │
           │ names ferryman, goat, cabbage, wolf and carry.               │
           │                                                              │
           │ Reply with your Python code inside one unique code block.    │
           │                                                              │
           │ ```python                                                    │
           │ class Module:                                                │
           │     """An abstract class to represent a UCLID5 module."""    │
           │                                                              │
           │     def types(self):                                         │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.         │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a  │
           │ 8-bit type called T:                                         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def types(self):                                     │
           │             self.T = BitVector(8)                            │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def locals(self):                                        │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their  │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ 8-bit variable x                                             │
           │         and an integer variable y:                           │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def locals(self):                                    │
           │             self.x = BitVector(8)                            │
           │             self.y = Integer()                               │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def inputs(self):                                        │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their  │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ input variable x,                                            │
           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by     │

           │ > You may recall the puzzle of a ferryman, goat, cabbage,    │
           │ and wolf all on one side of a river. The ferryman can cross  │
           │ the river with at most one passenger in his boat. There is a │
           │ behavioural conflict between: 1. the goat and the cabbage;   │
           │ and 2. the goat and the wolf; if they are on the same river  │
           │ bank but the ferryman crosses the river or stays on the      │
           │ other bank. We wish to know how the ferryman can transport   │
           │ all goods to the other side, without any conflicts           │
           │ occurring. Build a model in which the states represent which │
           │ goods are at which side of the river. Your model will need   │
           │ to have variables representing the location of the ferryman, │
           │ the goat, the cabbage and the wolf and a variable that       │
           │ indicates what is being carried in the ferry. Define a goal  │

           │ class Module:                                                │
           │     """An abstract class to represent a UCLID5 module."""    │
           │                                                              │
           │     def types(self):                                         │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.         │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a  │
           │ 8-bit type called T:                                         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def types(self):                                     │
           │             self.T = BitVector(8)                            │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def locals(self):                                        │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their  │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ 8-bit variable x                                             │
           │         and an integer variable y:                           │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def locals(self):                                    │
           │             self.x = BitVector(8)                            │
           │             self.y = Integer()                               │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def inputs(self):                                        │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their  │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ input variable x,                                            │
           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by     │
           │ 2-bit bitvectors:                                            │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def inputs(self):                                    │
           │             self.x = Array(BitVector(2), BitVector(8))       │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def outputs(self):                                       │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ output variable y,                                           │
           │         which is a real number:                              │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def outputs(self):                                   │
           │             self.y = Real()                                  │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def shared_vars(self):                                   │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the shared variables and their │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a  │
           │ shared variable z,                                           │
           │         which is an array of booleans indexed by integers:   │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def shared_vars(self):                               │
           │             self.z = Array(Integer(), Boolean())             │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def instances(self):                                     │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the instances of other modules │
           │ and relates their                                            │
           │         input, output, and shared variables to local         │
           │ variables. Every instance                                    │
           │         variable must be related to a local variable. For    │
           │ example, let M be                                            │
           │         another module with inputs x and y, and output z.    │
           │ The following                                                │
           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m,    │
           │ and connects M's                                             │
           │         input variable x to the local variable self.a, M's   │
           │ input variable y to                                          │
           │         the local variable self.b, and M's output variable z │
           │ to the local                                                 │
           │         variable self.c:                                     │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def instances(self):                                 │
           │             self.m = M(x=self.a, y=self.b, z=self.c)         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def init(self):                                          │
           │         """(Optional) Defines how variables are initialized. │
           │         For example, the following implementation            │
           │ initializes x to 0 if y is                                   │
           │         greater than or equal to 20:                         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def init(self):                                      │
           │             if self.y >= 20:                                 │
           │                 self.x = 0                                   │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def next(self):                                          │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the transition relation.       │
           │         For example, the following implementation increments │
           │ x or decrements y                                            │
           │         depending on z:                                      │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def next(self):                                      │

           │         pass                                                 │

           │                                                              │
           │         Returns:                                             │
           │             bool: True if the specification is satisfied,    │

           │ class FerryPuzzle(Module):                                   │
           │                                                              │
           │     def types(self):                                         │
           │         self.Location = Bit(1)                               │
           │                                                              │
           │     def locals(self):                                        │
           │         self.ferryman = self.Location                        │
           │         self.goat = self.Location                            │
           │         self.cabbage = self.Location                         │
           │         self.wolf = self.Location                            │
           │         self.carry = BitVector(2)                            │

           │     def init(self):                                          │
           │         self.ferryman = Bit(0)                               │

           │         self.cabbage = Bit(0)                                │
           │         self.wolf = Bit(0)                                   │
           │         self.carry = BitVecVal(0, 2)                         │
           │                                                              │
           │     def next(self):                                          │
           │         self.ferryman = 1 - self.ferryman                    │

           │                                                              │
           │     def types(self):                                         │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.         │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a  │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their  │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ 8-bit variable x                                             │
           │         and an integer variable y:                           │

           │         def locals(self):                                    │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by     │

           │         pass                                                 │

           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ output variable y,                                           │
           │         which is a real number:                              │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def outputs(self):                                   │

           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │

           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m,    │

           │ other bank. We wish to know how the ferryman can transport   │
           │ all goods to the other side, without any conflicts           │

           │ goods are at which side of the river. Your model will need   │
           │ to have variables representing the location of the ferryman, │
           │ the goat, the cabbage and the wolf and a variable that       │

           │ class Module:                                                │
           │     """An abstract class to represent a UCLID5 module."""    │
           │                                                              │
           │     def types(self):                                         │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.         │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a  │
           │ 8-bit type called T:                                         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def types(self):                                     │
           │             self.T = BitVector(8)                            │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def locals(self):                                        │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their  │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ 8-bit variable x                                             │
           │         and an integer variable y:                           │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def locals(self):                                    │
           │             self.x = BitVector(8)                            │
           │             self.y = Integer()                               │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def inputs(self):                                        │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their  │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ input variable x,                                            │
           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by     │
           │ 2-bit bitvectors:                                            │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def inputs(self):                                    │
           │             self.x = Array(BitVector(2), BitVector(8))       │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def outputs(self):                                       │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ output variable y,                                           │
           │         which is a real number:                              │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def outputs(self):                                   │
           │             self.y = Real()                                  │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def shared_vars(self):                                   │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the shared variables and their │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a  │
           │ shared variable z,                                           │
           │         which is an array of booleans indexed by integers:   │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def shared_vars(self):                               │
           │             self.z = Array(Integer(), Boolean())             │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def instances(self):                                     │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the instances of other modules │
           │ and relates their                                            │
           │         input, output, and shared variables to local         │
           │ variables. Every instance                                    │
           │         variable must be related to a local variable. For    │
           │ example, let M be                                            │
           │         another module with inputs x and y, and output z.    │
           │ The following                                                │
           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m,    │
           │ and connects M's                                             │
           │         input variable x to the local variable self.a, M's   │
           │ input variable y to                                          │
           │         the local variable self.b, and M's output variable z │
           │ to the local                                                 │
           │         variable self.c:                                     │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def instances(self):                                 │
           │             self.m = M(x=self.a, y=self.b, z=self.c)         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def init(self):                                          │
           │         """(Optional) Defines how variables are initialized. │
           │         For example, the following implementation            │
           │ initializes x to 0 if y is                                   │
           │         greater than or equal to 20:                         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def init(self):                                      │
           │             if self.y >= 20:                                 │
           │                 self.x = 0                                   │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def next(self):                                          │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the transition relation.       │
           │         For example, the following implementation increments │
           │ x or decrements y                                            │
           │         depending on z:                                      │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def next(self):                                      │
           │             if self.z:                                       │
           │                 self.x = self.x + 1                          │
           │             else:                                            │
           │                 self.y = self.y - 1                          │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │

           │     def specification(self):                                 │

           │                                                              │

           │ all goods to the other side, without any conflicts           │

           │                                                              │
           │     def types(self):                                         │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.         │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a  │
           │ 8-bit type called T:                                         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def types(self):                                     │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def locals(self):                                        │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their  │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ 8-bit variable x                                             │
           │         and an integer variable y:                           │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def locals(self):                                    │
           │             self.x = BitVector(8)                            │
           │             self.y = Integer()                               │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their  │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ input variable x,                                            │
           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by     │

           │         ```                                                  │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def outputs(self):                                       │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ output variable y,                                           │
           │         which is a real number:                              │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def outputs(self):                                   │
           │             self.y = Real()                                  │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │         which is an array of booleans indexed by integers:   │

           │         def shared_vars(self):                               │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def instances(self):                                     │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the instances of other modules │
           │ and relates their                                            │
           │         input, output, and shared variables to local         │
           │ variables. Every instance                                    │
           │         variable must be related to a local variable. For    │
           │ example, let M be                                            │
           │         another module with inputs x and y, and output z.    │
           │ The following                                                │
           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m,    │
           │ and connects M's                                             │
           │         input variable x to the local variable self.a, M's   │
           │ input variable y to                                          │
           │         the local variable self.b, and M's output variable z │
           │ to the local                                                 │
           │         variable self.c:                                     │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def instances(self):                                 │
           │             self.m = M(x=self.a, y=self.b, z=self.c)         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │ initializes x to 0 if y is                                   │
           │         greater than or equal to 20:                         │
           │         ```                                                  │

           │ > You may recall the puzzle of a ferryman, goat, cabbage,    │

           │ class Module:                                                │
           │     """An abstract class to represent a UCLID5 module."""    │
           │                                                              │
           │     def types(self):                                         │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.         │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a  │
           │ 8-bit type called T:                                         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def types(self):                                     │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def locals(self):                                        │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their  │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ 8-bit variable x                                             │
           │         and an integer variable y:                           │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def locals(self):                                    │
           │             self.x = BitVector(8)                            │
           │             self.y = Integer()                               │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their  │

           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by     │
           │ 2-bit bitvectors:                                            │

           │             self.x = Array(BitVector(2), BitVector(8))       │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def outputs(self):                                       │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ output variable y,                                           │
           │         which is a real number:                              │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def outputs(self):                                   │
           │             self.y = Real()                                  │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def shared_vars(self):                                   │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the shared variables and their │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def instances(self):                                     │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the instances of other modules │
           │ and relates their                                            │
           │         input, output, and shared variables to local         │
           │ variables. Every instance                                    │
           │         variable must be related to a local variable. For    │
           │ example, let M be                                            │
           │         another module with inputs x and y, and output z.    │
           │ The following                                                │
           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m,    │
           │ and connects M's                                             │
           │         input variable x to the local variable self.a, M's   │
           │ input variable y to                                          │
           │         the local variable self.b, and M's output variable z │
           │ to the local                                                 │
           │         variable self.c:                                     │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def instances(self):                                 │
           │             self.m = M(x=self.a, y=self.b, z=self.c)         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def init(self):                                          │
           │         """(Optional) Defines how variables are initialized. │
           │         For example, the following implementation            │
           │ initializes x to 0 if y is                                   │
           │         greater than or equal to 20:                         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def init(self):                                      │

           │         ```                                                  │

           │ class Module:                                                │
           │     """An abstract class to represent a UCLID5 module."""    │
           │                                                              │
           │     def types(self):                                         │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.         │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a  │
           │ 8-bit type called T:                                         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def types(self):                                     │
           │             self.T = BitVector(8)                            │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def locals(self):                                        │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their  │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ 8-bit variable x                                             │
           │         and an integer variable y:                           │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def locals(self):                                    │
           │             self.x = BitVector(8)                            │
           │             self.y = Integer()                               │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def inputs(self):                                        │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their  │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ input variable x,                                            │
           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by     │
           │ 2-bit bitvectors:                                            │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def inputs(self):                                    │
           │             self.x = Array(BitVector(2), BitVector(8))       │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def outputs(self):                                       │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ output variable y,                                           │
           │         which is a real number:                              │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def outputs(self):                                   │
           │             self.y = Real()                                  │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def shared_vars(self):                                   │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the shared variables and their │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a  │
           │ shared variable z,                                           │
           │         which is an array of booleans indexed by integers:   │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def shared_vars(self):                               │
           │             self.z = Array(Integer(), Boolean())             │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def instances(self):                                     │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the instances of other modules │
           │ and relates their                                            │
           │         input, output, and shared variables to local         │
           │ variables. Every instance                                    │
           │         variable must be related to a local variable. For    │
           │ example, let M be                                            │
           │         another module with inputs x and y, and output z.    │
           │ The following                                                │
           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m,    │
           │ and connects M's                                             │
           │         input variable x to the local variable self.a, M's   │
           │ input variable y to                                          │
           │         the local variable self.b, and M's output variable z │
           │ to the local                                                 │
           │         variable self.c:                                     │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def instances(self):                                 │
           │             self.m = M(x=self.a, y=self.b, z=self.c)         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def init(self):                                          │
           │         """(Optional) Defines how variables are initialized. │
           │         For example, the following implementation            │
           │ initializes x to 0 if y is                                   │
           │         greater than or equal to 20:                         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def init(self):                                      │
           │             if self.y >= 20:                                 │
           │                 self.x = 0                                   │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def next(self):                                          │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the transition relation.       │
           │         For example, the following implementation increments │
           │ x or decrements y                                            │
           │         depending on z:                                      │
           │         ```                                                  │

           │             if self.z:                                       │
           │                 self.x = self.x + 1                          │
           │             else:                                            │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def specification(self):                                 │
